=== battle 101
rules: Modern rules
format: Single
mine:
  mine#0 Palossand L14 Female Docile Water Compaction @Normal Gem | Sand Tomb, Astonish, Harden, Absorb | HP 48 Atk 26 Def 35 SpA 33 SpD 30 Spe 18
  mine#1 Mantyke L55 Male Lonely Swift Swim @nothing | Aqua Ring, Bounce, Take Down, Hydro Pump | HP 116 Atk 42 Def 59 SpA 77 SpD 141 Spe 66
  mine#2 Gyarados L54 Female Bashful Intimidate @Aguav Berry | Hydro Pump, Hurricane, Thrash, Hyper Beam | HP 170 Atk 141 Def 92 SpA 83 SpD 114 Spe 101
foe: Ace Trainer Tester, ai Basic, items none
  foe#0 Marowak L65 Female Quirky Rock Head @nothing | Endeavor, Bonemerang, Thrash, Double-Edge | HP 162 Atk 123 Def 167 SpA 82 SpD 114 Spe 75
  foe#1 Coalossal L32 Female Mild Steam Engine @nothing | Tackle, Rock Polish, Ancient Power, Incinerate | HP 112 Atk 62 Def 81 SpA 62 SpD 71 Spe 24
  foe#2 Claydol L11 Genderless Serious Levitate @nothing | Harden, Teleport, Hyper Beam, Rock Tomb | HP 35 Atk 22 Def 29 SpA 22 SpD 34 Spe 23
log:
  Seen Species=Marowak Form=none Gender=Female
  TrainersStand First=Ace Trainer Second=none
  say Ace Trainer Tester wants to battle!
  say Ace Trainer Tester sent out Marowak!
    + Entered E0 Pokemon=foe#0 Marowak FromBall=true
  say Go! Palossand!
    + Entered P0 Pokemon=mine#0 Palossand FromBall=true
  say Foe Marowak used Bonemerang!
    + Lunged E0 Category=Physical
    + MoveShown Move=Bonemerang Type=Ground Category=Physical From=E0 To=P0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
    ! Struck P0 Hp=0 Hard=false
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=false NotVeryEffective=false
  say Hit 1 time!
  say Palossand fainted!
    + Fainted P0
  say Go! Mantyke!
    + Entered P0 Pokemon=mine#1 Mantyke FromBall=true
  say Foe Marowak used Double-Edge!
    + Lunged E0 Category=Physical
    + MoveShown Move=Double-Edge Type=Normal Category=Physical From=E0 To=P0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
    ! Struck P0 Hp=0 Hard=false
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=false NotVeryEffective=false
  say Mantyke fainted!
    + Fainted P0
  say Go! Gyarados!
    + Entered P0 Pokemon=mine#2 Gyarados FromBall=true
  say Gyarados's Intimidate cuts Foe Marowak's Attack!
    + AbilityShown P0 Ability=Intimidate
  say Foe Marowak's Attack fell!
    + StageChanged E0 Stat=Attack Stage=-1 Rose=false
  say Gyarados used Hyper Beam!
    + Lunged P0 Category=Special
    + MoveShown Move=Hyper Beam Type=Normal Category=Special From=P0 To=E0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
    ! Struck E0 Hp=116 Hard=false
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=false NotVeryEffective=false
  say Foe Marowak used Double-Edge!
    + Lunged E0 Category=Physical
    + MoveShown Move=Double-Edge Type=Normal Category=Physical From=E0 To=P0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
    ! Struck P0 Hp=114 Hard=false
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=false NotVeryEffective=false
  say Gyarados must recharge!
  say Foe Marowak used Thrash!
    + Lunged E0 Category=Physical
    + MoveShown Move=Thrash Type=Normal Category=Physical From=E0 To=P0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
    ! Struck P0 Hp=58 Hard=false
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=false NotVeryEffective=false
  say Gyarados restored its health using its Aguav Berry!
    + ItemShown P0 Item=Aguav Berry
    + MomentShown P0 Moment=HeldItem From=none
    + HpChanged P0 Hp=79 Healed=true
  say Gyarados used Hurricane!
    + Lunged P0 Category=Special
    + MoveShown Move=Hurricane Type=Flying Category=Special From=P0 To=E0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
    ! Struck E0 Hp=67 Hard=false
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=false NotVeryEffective=false
  say Foe Marowak used Thrash!
    + Lunged E0 Category=Physical
    + MoveShown Move=Thrash Type=Normal Category=Physical From=E0 To=P0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
    ! Struck P0 Hp=19 Hard=false
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=false NotVeryEffective=false
  say Foe Marowak became confused due to fatigue!
    + Confused E0
    + VolatileChanged E0 Confused=true InLove=false
  say Gyarados used Hyper Beam!
    + Lunged P0 Category=Special
    + MoveShown Move=Hyper Beam Type=Normal Category=Special From=P0 To=E0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
    ! Struck E0 Hp=21 Hard=false
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=false NotVeryEffective=false
  say Foe Marowak is confused!
    + Confused E0
    + MomentShown E0 Moment=Confused From=none
  say It hurt itself in its confusion!
    ! Struck E0 Hp=10 Hard=false
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=false NotVeryEffective=false
  say Ace Trainer Tester withdrew Marowak!
    + Recalled E0
  Left E0 Ran=false
  say Ace Trainer Tester sent out Coalossal!
    + Seen Species=Coalossal Form=none Gender=Female
    + Entered E0 Pokemon=foe#1 Coalossal FromBall=true
  say Gyarados must recharge!
  say Gyarados used Thrash!

    ! Struck E0 Hp=66 Hard=false
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=false NotVeryEffective=true
  say It's not very effective...
  say Foe Coalossal used Tackle!
    + Lunged E0 Category=Physical
    + MoveShown Move=Tackle Type=Normal Category=Physical From=E0 To=P0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
    ! Struck P0 Hp=11 Hard=false
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=false NotVeryEffective=false

    + Lunged P0 Category=Physical
    + MoveShown Move=Thrash Type=Normal Category=Physical From=P0 To=E0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
    ! Struck E0 Hp=23 Hard=false
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=false NotVeryEffective=true
  say It's not very effective...
  say Foe Coalossal used Tackle!
    + Lunged E0 Category=Physical
    + MoveShown Move=Tackle Type=Normal Category=Physical From=E0 To=P0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
    ! Struck P0 Hp=3 Hard=false
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=false NotVeryEffective=false

    + Lunged P0 Category=Physical
    + MoveShown Move=Thrash Type=Normal Category=Physical From=P0 To=E0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
    ! Struck E0 Hp=0 Hard=false
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=false NotVeryEffective=true
  say It's not very effective...
  say Foe Coalossal fainted!

    + TrainerReacts E0 Cue=FirstDown
  say Gyarados gained 852 EXP. Points!

=== battle 102
rules: Modern rules
format: Double
mine:
  mine#0 Pupitar L37 Male Naive Shed Skin @nothing | Smack Down, Crunch, Earthquake, Stone Edge | HP 100 Atk 68 Def 67 SpA 54 SpD 56 Spe 56
  mine#1 Carracosta L50 Male Bold Solid Rock @nothing | Iron Defense, Aqua Tail, Rain Dance, Hydro Pump | HP 139 Atk 112 Def 152 SpA 89 SpD 78 Spe 39
  mine#2 Cloyster L32 Male Brave Shell Armor @Throat Spray | Ice Shard, Shell Smash, Icicle Crash, Razor Shell | HP 79 Atk 77 Def 122 SpA 67 SpD 37 Spe 46
foe: Ace Trainer Tester, ai Basic, items none
  foe#0 Oinkologne L39 Male Quiet Gluttony @nothing | Take Down, Yawn, Work Up, Uproar | HP 139 Atk 83 Def 75 SpA 67 SpD 75 Spe 59
  foe#1 Scovillain L53 Male Gentle Insomnia @Ring Target | Crunch, Seed Bomb, Overheat, Solar Beam | HP 137 Atk 123 Def 75 SpA 120 SpD 89 Spe 84
  foe#2 Terapagos L56 Female Naughty Tera Shift @Power Belt | Headbutt, Protect, Earth Power, Heavy Slam | HP 178 Atk 95 Def 112 SpA 91 SpD 102 Spe 78
log:
  Seen Species=Oinkologne Form=none Gender=Male
  Seen Species=Scovillain Form=none Gender=Male
  TrainersStand First=Ace Trainer Second=none
  say Ace Trainer Tester wants to battle!
  say Ace Trainer Tester sent out Oinkologne and Scovillain!
    + Entered E0 Pokemon=foe#0 Oinkologne FromBall=true
    + Entered E1 Pokemon=foe#1 Scovillain FromBall=true
  say Go! Pupitar and Carracosta!
    + Entered P0 Pokemon=mine#0 Pupitar FromBall=true
    + Entered P1 Pokemon=mine#1 Carracosta FromBall=true
  say Foe Scovillain used Seed Bomb!
    + Lunged E1 Category=Physical
    + MoveShown Move=Seed Bomb Type=Grass Category=Physical From=E1 To=P0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=true SuperEffective=true
    ! Struck P0 Hp=0 Hard=true
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=true NotVeryEffective=false
  say A critical hit!
  say It's super effective!
  say Pupitar fainted!
    + Fainted P0
  say Foe Oinkologne used Yawn!
    + Lunged E0 Category=Status
    + MoveShown Move=Yawn Type=Normal Category=Status From=E0 To=P1 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
  say Foe Oinkologne made Carracosta drowsy!
  say Carracosta used Rain Dance!
    + Lunged P1 Category=Status
    + MoveShown Move=Rain Dance Type=Water Category=Status From=P1 To=P1 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
  say It started to rain!
    + WeatherChanged Weather=Rain
  say Rain continues to fall.
    + WeatherContinues Weather=Rain
  say Go! Cloyster!
    + Entered P0 Pokemon=mine#2 Cloyster FromBall=true
  say Foe Scovillain used Seed Bomb!
    + Lunged E1 Category=Physical
    + MoveShown Move=Seed Bomb Type=Grass Category=Physical From=E1 To=P0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=true
    ! Struck P0 Hp=0 Hard=true
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=true NotVeryEffective=false
  say It's super effective!
  say Cloyster fainted!
    + Fainted P0
  say Foe Oinkologne used Work Up!
    + Lunged E0 Category=Status
    + MoveShown Move=Work Up Type=Normal Category=Status From=E0 To=E0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
  say Foe Oinkologne's Attack rose!
    + StageChanged E0 Stat=Attack Stage=1 Rose=true
  say Foe Oinkologne's Sp. Atk rose!
    + StageChanged E0 Stat=SpAttack Stage=1 Rose=true
  say Carracosta used Hydro Pump!
    + Lunged P1 Category=Special
    + MoveShown Move=Hydro Pump Type=Water Category=Special From=P1 To=E1 Missed=true Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
  say Foe Scovillain avoided the attack!
  say Rain continues to fall.
    + WeatherContinues Weather=Rain
  say Carracosta fell asleep!
    + StatusChanged P1 Status=Sleep
  say Foe Scovillain used Seed Bomb!
    + Lunged E1 Category=Physical
    + MoveShown Move=Seed Bomb Type=Grass Category=Physical From=E1 To=P1 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=true
    ! Struck P1 Hp=1 Hard=true
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=true NotVeryEffective=false
  say It's super effective!
  say Foe Oinkologne used Take Down!
    + Lunged E0 Category=Physical
    + MoveShown Move=Take Down Type=Normal Category=Physical From=E0 To=P1 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
    ! Struck P1 Hp=0 Hard=false
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=false NotVeryEffective=true
  say It's not very effective...
  say Foe Oinkologne is hit with recoil!
    + HpChanged E0 Hp=138 Healed=false
  say Carracosta fainted!
    + Fainted P1
  say Lucas has no Pokémon left that can fight!
  say ...  ...  ...
  say Lucas whited out!
  Ended Result=PlayerDefeat
result: PlayerDefeat after 2 turns
